NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Fern Benally • Alberto L. Peshlakai •Jason E. Whiting • Daryl Seymore • Dawnafe
Whitesinger
“We are Navajo County”
 
NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
Thursday, July 30, 2026 *SPECIAL MEETING
NAVAJO COUNTY GOVERNMENTAL COMPLEX
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS' CHAMBERS
100 EAST CODE TALKERS DRIVE
HOLBROOK, AZ 86025
ALL SUPERVISORS WILL ATTEND VIRTUALLY
Pursuant to A.R.S. §38-431.02(H), the public body will have physical acces to the meeting place one (1) hour prior
to the start of the meeting.
 
The Board may vote to meet in a closed executive session to discuss certain matters and for legal advice on any item
as indicated on the agenda, pursuant to A.R.S. §38-431.03(A)(3). Items on the agenda may be considered out of
order at the Chairperson's discretion. A copy of the agenda background material provided to the Board, except for
items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection on the Navajo County
website, www.navajocountyaz.gov
Microsoft Teams meeting
Join: https://teams.microsoft.com/meet/275380126471493?p=V7kGxzVxX6g671jPVv 
Meeting ID: 275 380 126 471 493
Passcode: MU6CZ6sY
Dial in by phone
+1 623-232-2568,,846051763# United States, Agua Fria
Find a local number 
Phone conference ID: 846 051 763#
All public comments will need to be made in person or in writing. Written comments will be received by the Clerk of
the Board's office twenty-four (24) hours prior to the Board meeting, at leah.thomas@navajocountyaz.gov. 
Individuals addressing the Board when an item is called are also limited to three minutes, however, additional time
may be allotted by the Chairman to address any specific questions asked by members of the Board.
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of
the Board’s office at (928) 524-4053, at least 48 hours prior to the meeting, so accommodation can be arranged.
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: 
           
1. ELECTIONS: 
 
a. Consideration and approval of the Official Canvass of the results for the July 21,
2026, Primary Election as presented. 
Presented By: Rayleen Richards

<!-- page 2 -->
2. ADJOURN 
 
Dated: July 28, 2026 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
Posted: 2:45 p.m. By: lt                         
Leah Thomas - Clerk of the Board

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisors Special 1. a.        
Meeting Date: 07/30/2026  
Title: Canvass of the July 21, 2026 Primary Election 
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Presented By: Rayleen Richards
Motion before the Board:
Consideration and approval of the Official Canvass of the results for the July 21, 2026, Primary
Election as presented.
Background:
Pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-642
Canvass of election; postponements 
The governing body holding an election shall meet and canvass the election not less than
six days nor more than twenty days following the election.
1.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval
